AGENT COORDINATION
Saving Constantinople development instructions and handovers
Snapshot: 5 November2026 — Version41 published; approved Theodoro follow-up verified for next publication

AGENT INSTRUCTIONS

Game identity, confirmed 4 November 2026: full title Against Fate: Saving Constantinople; short name Saving Constantinople. Use these in all new player-facing material and publications. Preserve historical release links, asset provenance, save extensions, preference keys, package identifiers and the existing GitHub repository address. These are compatibility identifiers, not current branding.

Every developer reads this record before work, then verifies the current branch, dirty files, active assignments and build ownership. ConstantineX leads integration. User instructions override this coordination record. Work in bounded segments under 15 minutes; report meaningful progress and blockers.

Begin every user-facing update and agent message: This is [workstream designation] â€” [HH:mm, date, timezone]. Update BLUF: [one plain-language sentence stating the outcome or request]. Use Europe/Bucharest time, verified when posting. Then give only the details needed to act. Identify uncertainty and the next owner. Occasional humour and individual personality are welcome; keep decisions and evidence unambiguous.

Claim exact files or methods before editing. One owner per shared change. Use separate staging files or branches for independent work; agree on integration before touching another ownerâ€™s area. Never revert unknown changes. Do not assume a delivered message was acknowledged.

One Unity build owner and one release/version owner at a time. Handover states version, build path, process completion, tests and unresolved issues. Never overwrite an active capture player. Preserve saves. Commit only reviewed owned files.

Use evidence labels: proposed, approved, staged, installed, compiled, runtime checked, visually reviewed, committed, pushed, desktop published. None implies the next. Report checks and their scope; test counts alone do not establish gameplay quality.

ConstantineX maintains this document and its text companion from the same content. Other workstreams send updates rather than concurrently editing the Word binary. Refresh at handover, scope change, blocker, design decision and release. Replace stale current assignments; retain concise decisions and lessons. Future agents verify snapshots before acting.

Recovery after failure: mark the workstream inactive, preserve its files and branches, inspect unfinished changes, then explicitly reassign ownership. Never assume an inactive agent completed or abandoned its edits.

Efficiency: batch independent reads; test the changed behaviour first; reuse passing evidence until changes invalidate it. Avoid repeated status polling, duplicate research and speculative subsystems. Keep simulation separate from presentation, preserve stable IDs and save compatibility, and leave sources/ read-only.

Every publication includes a player-readable feature and change list, validation scope, known unfinished items and the actual build location. Update VERSION CHANGELOG in this guide at publication. The final INTERAGENT MESSAGE LOG preserves original coordination messages with source evidence; never rewrite old messages to match later outcomes. Keep historical messages distinct from current instructions.

Disciplined engineering: state the outcome and acceptance criteria, then stay on that objective; put unrelated discoveries in the backlog. Keep a short prioritised queue and deliver small, complete chunks. Delegate bounded tasks with explicit ownership and handovers. Search first and read selectively. Use proportional, focused checks; stop when they pass unless new evidence justifies more. After two attempts without progress, diagnose the cause and change method or escalate the blocker. Leave a compact checkpoint of completed work, state, evidence and next action. Report evidence-based outcomes and limits. Continue autonomously within authorised scope; ask only for decisions or information that materially affect the result.

Asset creation and integration: follow Documentation/Art assets and presentation standard.txt. Event art prefers 2048x1152 landscape PNG with an 8% composition safe zone; existing sizes remain valid. Use the existing event manifest, contain by default, and the renderer's inset clipped viewport; never stretch imagery or bake UI/borders into artwork. Icons usually use 512-square transparent masters; court figures usually 1024x1536 with consistent floor alignment. Preserve stable paths/meta GUIDs. Run Tools/validate_event_art.py and focused visual/resize checks before publication. Match the present art direction; stage incomplete assets/code outside Assets during another owner's build.

Art direction: no minarets in pre-conquest Constantinople, around Hagia Sophia or in the surrounding countryside shown in this game. Modern Istanbul reference imagery must be corrected. Check skylines explicitly when generating or integrating art.

Asset provenance: maintain Documentation/ASSET SOURCES.md as the central index. Link existing manifests. Record source, creator, licence, attribution, modifications and integration status for new artwork/audio; do not equate a free download with commercial redistribution permission.


ARCHITECTURE

Verified entry points in the Unity checkout: VariableSingleton stores campaign values and exposes CaptureState and restore paths. SaveGames handles campaign saves. CouncilActions changes campaign state and uses EventOutcome for results. These are existing integration points; avoid parallel state stores.

CouncilUI and CampaignHUD present the campaign; ControlSingleton handles return navigation. Check both screen visibility and return callbacks when adding a nested menu. Presentation must not consume simulation randomness or alter campaign state.

OttomanDirector is the strategic decision entry point. Event choices, diplomacy, provincial systems and assault outcomes feed persistent campaign values. Changes affecting those values require save/load and downstream outcome checks. This is an entry-point map, not a completed dependency audit.

New CouncilChamber is decorative UI, loaded from Resources with editable JSON candle anchors. It is clipped and non-interactive, pauses when hidden and supports still presentation. Its capture checks cover campaign/RNG isolation and navigation.

The campaign Decision Journal was published in Version 37: daily notes and persistent decision records, a council entry, and save-state inclusion. It is separate from the assault journal. Our Turks decision records are integrated in Version 39.1; final epilogue integration remains future work.

Version 38 uses SharedProvisions as the common physical-food ledger for city, Ottoman camp, provincial reserves and convoy cargo, preserving fractions through existing campaign values. ProvincialWar debits reserves at departure and transfers surviving cargo on arrival. Medical treatment adds its selected food charge. Version 38 compiled, passed 149 focused runtime checks and was desktop published; a full long-campaign balance pass remains future work.

Event artwork is manifest-driven through EventCardArt and EventArtworkView. The picture lives in an inset RectMask2D viewport; gold framing remains outside that mask. Contain/cover and enlarged-view behaviour remain unchanged. Asset generation specifications do not add game mechanics, parallel asset registries or a new loading system.

Version41: CountryDiplomacy adds gazaria at index31 without shifting earlier IDs; caffa aliases now resolve there. Ottoman candar province remains Paphlagonia; Sinop is a separate court marker. Ragusa service reserves troops abroad and removes their daily city rations; survivors use TrainingPool expeditionReturn. Copa uses saved per-voyage cargo and SharedProvisions reserve; its mercenary definition index20 reuses existing contract lifecycle. No second country or food system.


DECISIONS

5 November2026 confirmed: Copa trading terms100 provisions OR350gold weekly, finite2400 reserve,14-day baseline voyage, existing hazards/closed-strait departure hold. Theodoro approved provisional once-only terms: +10goodwill free;1500gold for multiplicative+5% total archer lethality; DismountedChampions unlocks80cavalry for10800gold via existing sea contract (half advance/half arrival). No reincorporation or Bank takeovers.

Discovery achievements (Alchemical Genius) may unlock in Normal or Ironman; performance achievements remain Ironman-only and automated/practice sessions unlock neither. Rewards are independent, once per campaign: alchemy +1 secret, pigeon letter +1 secret, treasure +2500 gold/+1 secret, fossil skull journal only. Unfinished alchemy resets on leaving or 60 seconds inactivity. Bronze ring drags the desk horizontally or switches sides on click.

Confirmed by user: ConstantineX leads the taskgroup. OrbanX is inactive after a critical error and must not receive new assignments. JohanX remains an active workstream. Rationale: maintain one accountable integration owner and explicit recovery.

Confirmed by user: keep this record updated under AGENT INSTRUCTIONS, ARCHITECTURE, DECISIONS and COORDINATION. Use named, timestamped BLUF updates. Rationale: readable context survives chat loss and future handovers.

Confirmed art direction: council centre uses an atmospheric Byzantine desk chamber, broad city-facing windows and informally arranged objects, with gentle candle animation. Keep existing council navigation and quotation presentation.

Confirmed standing gameplay decisions: factions, merchant favour and general engineer mentorship are retired. Population counts individuals. Ironman earns career achievements; normal campaigns do not. The newer shared-food decision below supersedes the earlier nearest-thousand food-consumption rule for Version 38.

Implementation protocol chosen by ConstantineX: one editor regenerates Word and a diffable text companion from one source. This is an operational convention, not a new gameplay rule. Proposed mechanics remain labelled proposals until approved.

Confirmed 3 November: publish the present integrated update as Version 37; use whole version numbers for major updates. Reserve unfinished changes for Version 38. The standalone Albanian war test is retired; the provincial war framework remains authoritative. Decision Journal occupies the council location formerly used for Development Options; development tools move into the campaign menu.

Version 38 approved shared-food model: one provision feeds 1,000 people for one day, preserving fractional demand and stock. Provincial reserves are finite. A fall in Wealth destroys stored food in the same proportion: remaining reserve equals the previous reserve multiplied by new Wealth divided by previous Wealth. Fortnightly shipments debit real cargo from reserves; raids and deliveries affect that actual cargo. Continued food deprivation escalates its consequences rather than remaining a flat repeated interaction.

Provisional Version 38 balancing assumptions: summer harvest runs Juneâ€“August; total opening provincial reserve is 13,200 provisions. These are tuning values pending playtesting, not final historical claims.

Confirmed medical-food decision: charge one extra provision per 20 patients actually treated per day, in addition to ordinary population consumption. This supersedes the earlier no-extra-charge assumption; implementation and verification must follow the treated-patient count.

Map continuity: Paphlagonia remains Ottoman-controlled under the existing approved simplification. A separate Sinop marker for Candar is a proposal only; staged Candar work does not authorise changing the province owner.

4 November 2026: preserve the existing event presentation and adopt per-asset authoring guidance rather than one mandatory dimension for every image. Prefer 16:9 new event art, protect focal content with safe margins, accept legacy aspect ratios using contain. Shared event-image clipping must be validated at 16:9, 16:10, 4:3 and ultrawide PC shapes; full-game certification is separate.

4 November 2026: Technologies uses a compact catalogue and one selected technology detail with artwork, effect, historical flavour and existing research cost/action. Preserve TechnologySystem rules and stable artwork keys. New campaigns allocate the initial monk roster equally between infirmary and research (odd remainder in care); restoring saved allocations never reapplies this default. Additional recruits still join care under existing rules.

Release 39 scope: archive older Emperor XP, court-quest and murder-investigation prototypes, as explicitly confirmed by the user. Preserve Candar, Ragusa and Malta DLC outside live Assets. Do not revive retired factions, fencing or mentorship during consolidation.

4 November 2026: user selected button-lighting concept B, Warm side lanterns. Shared mosaic buttons use rounded amber light pools at left/right ends, fading before the centre. Existing keyboard selection, disabled-state handling, captions and click targets are preserved.

4 November 2026: technology-room discoveries approved. Independent once-per-campaign letter, treasure and alchemy rewards; treasure 2,500 gold + one secret. Unfinished alchemy resets on leaving or 60 seconds inactivity. Discovery achievements work in Normal and Ironman; campaign achievements remain Ironman-only; automated tests unlock neither. Bronze-ring horizontal panel dragging uses a gripping-hand cursor. Full confirmed rules: Documentation/CursorStudies/DISCOVERY-AND-CURSOR-DECISIONS.txt. Not yet implemented.

Senate, confirmed 5 November 2026: title The Senate; reconstructed Blachernae-inspired assembly, emperor listening centrally, five groups including Galata envoy with two black-clad guards and Genoese cross banner. Consistent groups now, low-support variants deferred. Presentation only: preserve support actions and Galata rules.
